## LU-KYÜÔ DJÜN FOH-ING SHÜ. <u>路加</u>传福音书

### Lk. I.

<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>KYI-JÜN</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>好几个</rb><rt>hao-kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>*cong*</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>笃实</rb><rt>toh-jih</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>件打件</rb><rt>gyin-tang-gyin</rt><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>传道个</rb><rt>djün-dao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>亲眼</rb><rt>tsʽing-ngæn</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt><rb>传落来</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从头硬节</rb><rt>dzong-deo-ngeng-tsih</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>仔细</rb><rt>ts-si</rt><rb>查过</rb><rt>dzô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>挨次序</rb><rt>a-tsʽ-jü</rt><rb>写出来</rb><rt>sia-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>提阿非罗</rb><rt>Di-o-fi-lo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>da-jing</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>学过个</rb><rt>ʽôh-ko-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>HYI-LIH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>亚比亚</rb><rt>Üô-pi-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伦</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-leng-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>礼节</rb><rt>li-tsih</rt><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Pi* *da-ka* *eo* “*Yiæ-su* *kyüông-seng* *keh* *nyin*,” *tsao* *loh* *nyin*. 比大概讴「<u>耶稣</u>降生箇年」早六年。（原注）</sup>

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>挨班个</rb><rt>a-pæn-go</rt><rb>次序</rb><rt>tsʽ-jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>祭司个</rb><rt>tsi-s-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抽签</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽin</rt><rb>抽着</rb><rt>tsʽiu-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>点</rb><rt>tin</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>点</rb><rt>Tin</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>香坛个</rb><rt>hyiang-dæn-go</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>懔懔动</rb><rt>ling-ling-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔自</rb><rt>Ng-zi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生出来</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Dziu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老酒</rb><rt>lao-tsiu</rt><rb>烧酒</rb><rt>siao-tsiu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>胎</rb><rt>tʽæ</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>满足了</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>归服</rb><rt>kwe-voh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>性情</rb><rt>sing-dzing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依顺个</rb><rt>i-jing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>获</rb><rt>ʽôh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>一班</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大了</rb><rt>do-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>*yiu-go*</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加百列</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>却勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做成个</rb><rt>tso-dzing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>侍候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>耽搁间</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>走出来了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>装</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>手势</rb><rt>siu-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>竟是介</rb><rt>kying-z-ka</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 侍候：等候</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>临满</rb><rt>ling-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>寻寻</rb><rt>zing-zing</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照顾</rb><rt>tsiao-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*ngô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>TAO</rt><rb>第六个</rb><rt>di-loh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加百列</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省</rb><rt>*sang*</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家个</rb><rt>keh-kô-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大姑娘</rb><rt>do-kwu-nyiang</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>Teh-djôh</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恭喜</rb><rt>kong-hyi</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>𢖺𢖺动</rb><rt>yiah-yiah-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>？

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>受胎</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇族</rb><rt>keh-dzoh</rt><rb>世世代代个</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>末</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>遮荫</rb><rt>tsô-ing</rt><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>*Na-hwun*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身孕</rb><rt>sing-yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六个</rb><rt>loh-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>别开</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省</rb><rt>*sang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>*sing-hyiang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>胎</rb><rt>tʽæ</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳起来</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>满足了</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>，<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>胎里</rb><rt>tʽæ-li</rt><rb>生出来个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>*hao-cʽü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>望望</rb><rt>mông-mông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>耳朵里</rb><rt>ng-to-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>胎</rb><rt>tʽæ</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>跳起来</rb><rt>tʽiao-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>*Cü-go*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。<sup> </sup>」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Ng siang-sing Cü-go shih-wô yiu yiao-nyiæn, tao yiu foh-kyʽi. 或话，尔相信主个说话有效验，倒有福气。（原注）</sup>

<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，

「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>灵魂</rb><rt>ling-weng</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>低微个</rb><rt>ti-vi-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>Pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>手梗</rb><rt>siu-kwang</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>劲道</rb><rt>gying-dao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>骄傲</rb><rt>kyiao-ngao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>七丁八倒</rb><rt>tsʽih-ting-pah-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>推</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>低微</rb><rt>ti-vi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>举</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>Du-kyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>富足个</rb><rt>fu-tsoh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>空手</rb><rt>kʽong-siu</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>记念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>当初</rb><rt>*tông-tsʽu*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>道个</rb><rt>dao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>扶助</rb><rt>vu-dzu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>56</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>Yi-li-sô-pah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大约</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>庉了</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>57</sup> <u><ruby><rb>以利沙伯</rb><rt>YI-LI-SÔ-PAH</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生姆</rb><rt>sang-m</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>Ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>欢喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>60</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>61</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>62</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>装装</rb><rt>tsông-tsông</rt><rb>手势</rb><rt>siu-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*ah-tia*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>粉板</rb><rt>feng-pæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>写出来</rb><rt>sia-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。」 <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>64</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>舌头</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>开开</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲起来</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝赞</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>怕起来</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>各到处</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>话开去了</rb><rt>wô-kʽæ-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>记勒</rb><rt>kyi-leh</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>亲手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>朵化</rb><rt>tô-hwô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>67</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒迦利亚</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>受了</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>满足了</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，

<sup>68</sup> 「<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>Kæ</rt><rb>祝赞</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<sup>69</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇家</rb><rt>keh-kô</rt><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>救世个</rb><rt>kyiu-shü-go</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<sup>70</sup> （<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>），<sup>71</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>72</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>记念</rb><rt>kyi-nyiæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>圣善个</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<sup>73</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>罚过个</rb><rt>vah-ko-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<sup>74</sup> <ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>仇敌个</rb><rt>dziu-dih-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>75</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>清洁</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih</rt><rb>一生一世</rb><rt>ih-sang-ih-si</rt><rb>放胆</rb><rt>fông-tæn</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *I-s* *ziu-z*, ih-go yiu neng-ken go Kyiu-cü. 意思就是，一个有能干个救主。（原注）</sup>

<sup>76</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<sup>77</sup> <ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>救个</rb><rt>kyiu-go</rt><rb>方法</rb><rt>fông-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>78</sup> <ruby><rb>亏勒</rb><rt>Kyʽü-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>慈悲</rb><rt>dz-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>出去个</rb><rt>cʽih-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>日光</rb><rt>Nyih-kwông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<sup>79</sup> <ruby><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>阴司</rb><rt>ing-s</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>遮暗</rb><rt>tsô-en</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>笔直</rb><rt>pih-dzih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>太平个</rb><rt>tʽa-bing-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng-go* *z-ngæn* *yiu-teh* *u-sen* *fæn* Tsʽô-hong; kʽen Y. 11. 1. Sk. 3. 8: 6. 12. 本文个字眼有得乌碎翻岔蕻；看 Y. 11. 1. Sk. 3. 8: 6. 12.（原注）</sup>

<sup>80</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>多起来</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>。

### Lk. II.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>亚古士督</rb><rt>Üô-kwu-z-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>上谕</rb><rt>zông-yü</rt><rb>颁落来</rb><rt>pæn-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>天下人</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô-nying</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>挨户册</rb><rt>a-wu-tsʽah</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> （<u><ruby><rb>居里扭</rb><rt>Kyü-li-nyiu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙利亚</rb><rt>Jü-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>挨户册</rb><rt>a-wu-tsʽah</rt><rb>刚刚</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>本城</rb><rt>peng-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>册子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4, 5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>族里</rb><rt>dzoh-li</rt><rb>箇派</rb><rt>keh-pʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>定间个</rb><rt>ding-kæn-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>大肚个</rb><rt>do-du-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省</rb><rt>*sang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>册子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉间</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生姆</rb><rt>sang-m</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>头一个</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>裹好仔</rb><rt>ko-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>困勒</rb><rt>kwʽeng-leh</rt><rb>马槽里</rb><rt>mô-zao-li</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>下处里</rb><rt>ʽô-cʽü-li</rt><rb>呒采</rb><rt>m-tsʽæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉了</rb><rt>deng-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>Keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>看羊个</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>夜头</rb><rt>yia-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天外</rb><rt>tʽin-nga</rt><rb>调班</rb><rt>diao-pæn</rt><rb>巡更</rb><rt>jing-kang</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>Hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>吓倒了</rb><rt>hah-tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>欢喜个</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>今末子</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>生出来</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>布</rb><rt>pu</rt><rb>裹的</rb><rt>ko-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>马槽里</rb><rt>mô-zao-li</rt><rb>困间</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>记认</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>Hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>兵将</rb><rt>ping-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>Wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>*tʽin*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, læ-tih kwu-djôh. 或话，来的顾着。（原注）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看羊个</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>马槽里</rb><rt>mô-zao-li</rt><rb>困间个</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>看过仔</rb><rt>Kʽen-ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>*tʽin-s*</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>话出来了</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>看羊个</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>存的</rb><rt>dzeng-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>看羊个</rb><rt>Kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>*tʽin-s*</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>依路</rb><rt>*i-lu*</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>受胎</rb><rt>ziu-tʽæ</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>取</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>TSIAO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>洁净个</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>满了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> （<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>律法里</rb><rt>lih-fah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>头一胎</rb><rt>deo-ih-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-go</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>献</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽOh-tsia</rt><rb>一双</rb><rt>ih-shông</rt><rb>斑鸠</rb><rt>pæn-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>鹁鸽</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>安慰</rb><rt>en-w̆e</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>暗暗</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>*Si-min*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>圣灵个</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling-go</rt><rb>感动</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>走进来</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法个</rb><rt>lih-fah-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>抱仔</rb><rt>bao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝赞</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，

<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>安安稳稳</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>去世</rb><rt>kyʽü-shü</rt></ruby>，<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>亲眼</rb><rt>tsʽing-ngæn</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>万国</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>shih-lih-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>点着</rb><rt>tin-djôh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*Ng-ts*</rt><rb>立落来</rb><rt>lih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt></ruby>，<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>露出来</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>宝剑</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>穿过</rb><rt>cʽün-ko</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚设</rb><rt>Üô-sih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇派</rb><rt>keh-pʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>法内力</u>个</rb><rt>Fah-ne-lih-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老老了</rb><rt>lao-lao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>新妇</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>后</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>凑队</rb><rt>tsʽeo-de</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>八十</rb><rt>pah-jih</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>赎罪</rb><rt>joh-ze</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>*Iah-seh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>*Mô-li-üô*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>做好</rb><rt>tso-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>省</rb><rt>*sang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>Na-hwun</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>多起来</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>足足个了</rb><rt>tsoh-tsoh-go-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>每年</rb><rt>ME-NYIN</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>节肯个</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>满了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>Siao-nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>耽搁间</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>Tsih</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>走勒</rb><rt>tseo-leh</rt><rb>一日个</rb><rt>ih-nyih-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>熟人</rb><rt>joh-nying</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒走转</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 8. 耶（稣）后（头）8（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>过勒</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一面</rb><rt>ih-min</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一面</rb><rt>ih-min</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>答应个</rb><rt>teh-ing-go</rt><rb>灵巧</rb><rt>*ling-kyʽiao*</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>*Do-nying*</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>Siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>寻勒</rb><rt>zing-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>伤心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>寻</rb><rt>Zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>记勒</rb><rt>kyi-leh</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>多起来</rb><rt>to-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>越弄</rb><rt>yüih-long</rt><rb>越</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, nying do-kyʽi-læ. 或话，人大起来。（原注）</sup>

### Lk. III.

<u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>KÆ-SAH</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>提庇留</rb><rt>Di-pi-liu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>第十五</rb><rt>di-jih-ng</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，（<u><ruby><rb>本丢</rb><rt>Peng-tiu</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>tsong-toh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以土利亚</rb><rt>Yi-tʽu-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>特拉可尼提</rb><rt>Deh-lah-kʽo-nyi-di</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方个</rb><rt>di-fông-go</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>吕撒聂</rb><rt>Li-sah-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚比利尼</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pi-li-nyi-go</rt><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该亚法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒迦利亚</u>个</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>四凸乱圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>回心转意个</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以赛亚</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô-go</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>Kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>铺直</rb><rt>pʽu-dzih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>街道</rb><rt>ka-dao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>山里个</rb><rt>Sæn-li-go</rt><rb>岙</rb><rt>ao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>填勒</rb><rt>din-leh</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>岭</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弯曲个</rb><rt>w̆æn-kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>蹊跷个</rb><rt>kyʽi-kyʽiao-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>平</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血气</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>*Iah-ʽen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>毒蛇个</rb><rt>Doh-dzô-go</rt><rb>种党</rb><rt>cong-tông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>指引</rb><rt>ts-ying</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>将来个</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>？ <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>Tsæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>斧头</rb><rt>fu-deo</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>根头</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>斫倒</rb><rt>tsôh-tao</rt><rb>丢勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>Keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？」 <sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>两件</rb><rt>liang-gyin</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>一件</rb><rt>ih-gyin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？」 <sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>额外</rb><rt>Ngah-nga</rt><rb>钱粮</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>吃钱粮</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>*Iah-ʽen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>把势</rb><rt>pô-shü</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>谎告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>索诈</rb><rt>soh-tsô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>钱粮</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>知足</rb><rt>cü-tsoh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, M-nao hah nying-kô. 或话，呒得好吓人家。（原注）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>*Kyi-toh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>鞋带</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>粪箕</rb><rt>feng-kyi</rt><rb>驮的</rb><rt>do-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>扬</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>晒场里个</rb><rt>sa-dziang-li-go</rt><rb>谷</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>谷</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收进</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>仓里</rb><rt>tsʽông-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>糠验</rb><rt>kʽông-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>熓</rb><rt>u</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>烧掉</rb><rt>siao-diao</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>*Iah-ʽen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>劝化</rb><rt>kyʽün-hwô</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih. 或话，疑惑不决。（原注）</sup>

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>藩王</rb><rt>væn-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力</u>个</rb><rt>Fi-lih-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希罗底</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>做过个</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt></ruby>；<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>关</rb><rt>kwæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 30. 耶（稣）后（头）30（年）。（原注）</sup>

<u><ruby><rb>拉麦</rb><rt>*Lah-mah*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛土撒拉</rb><rt>Mô-tʽu-sah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>玛土撒拉</rb><rt>*Mô-tʽu-sah-lah*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以诺</rb><rt>Yi-nôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以诺</rb><rt>*Yi-nôh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>Yüô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅列</rb><rt>*Yüô-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>玛勒列</rb><rt>Mô-leh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>玛勒列</rb><rt>*Mô-leh-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该南</rb><rt>Kæ-nen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>该南</rb><rt>*Kæ-nen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以挪士</rb><rt>Yi-no-z</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以挪士</rb><rt>*Yi-no-z*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>设</u>个</rb><rt>Shih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>设</rb><rt>*Shih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚当</rb><rt>Üô-tông</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚当</rb><rt>*Üô-tông*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>。
