<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>服</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>*eo*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>跟间个</rb><rt>keng-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>Ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>碰见过</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>差去个</rb><rt>Tsʽa-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>生病个</rb><rt>sang-bing-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>好东了</rb><rt>hao-tong-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿因</rb><rt>Nô-ing</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>好几个</rb><rt>hao-kyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>Kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城门口</rb><rt>dzing-meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>抬出来</rb><rt>dæ-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>抬个</rb><rt>dæ-go</rt><rb>架子</rb><rt>kô-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>抬个</rb><rt>dæ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立落</rb><rt>lih-lôh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>后生</rb><rt>ʽEo-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐起来</rb><rt>zo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲起</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>出来了</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看顾</rb><rt>kʽen-kwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>扬开</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>四凸乱圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>各处</rb><rt>kôh-cʽü</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh* *wô*, kwun-zæ. 或话，棺材。（原注）</sup>

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>IAH-ʽEN-GO</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>Tông-kæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>？』」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大小</rb><rt>do-siao</rt><rb>病症</rb><rt>bing-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>犯</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt><rb>听闻过</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>报</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聋𦗭</rb><rt>long-bang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>转去</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讲起</rb><rt>kông-kyʽi</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>闲早</rb><rt>ʽæn-tsao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>吹动</rb><rt>cʽü-dong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>芦竿</rb><rt>lu-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>软相</rb><rt>nyün-siang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>华丽</rb><rt>wô-li</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>酒肉</rb><rt>tsiu-nyüoh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>王宫</rb><rt>wông-kong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>Z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>*sin-cü-nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>过如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>打发</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>前头个</rb><rt>zin-deo-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。 <sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神国里</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>弃掉了</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh* *wô*, pô Jing-ming-go ts-i kyʽi-diao-de, z-ka zi ʽæ zi. 或话，把神明个旨意弃掉了，是介自害自。（原注）</sup>

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>Kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>小娃</rb><rt>siao-w̆æn</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>讴来讴去</rb><rt>eo-læ-eo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>笛子</rb><rt>dih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>跳舞</rb><rt>tʽiao-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt></ruby>。』<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>贪食</rb><rt>tʽen-zih</rt><rb>贪酒个</rb><rt>tʽen-tsiu-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>罪人个</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>*Cü-kwu-go*</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>Zing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>坏人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>𣥼的个</rb><rt>tsi-tih-go</rt><rb>玉个</rb><rt>nyüoh-go</rt><rb>盒子</rb><rt>ʽeh-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>亲脚沿</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyiah-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>淋湿</rb><rt>ling-sih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>揩揩</rb><rt>kʽa-kʽa</rt><rb>燥</rb><rt>sao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>嗅嗅</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>脚里</rb><rt>*kyiah-li*</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>摸</rb><rt>moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>坯子</rb><rt>pʽe-ts</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坏人</rb><rt>w̆a-nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一句</rb><rt>ih-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*ziu*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>债主</rb><rt>tsa-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>债</rb><rt>tsa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>*kyʽin*</rt><rb>五</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>*liang*</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>债主</rb><rt>*tsa-cü*</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>债主</rb><rt>tsa-cü</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>？」<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>猜</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看勒</rb><rt>kʽen-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>错</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>扭带转</rb><rt>nyin-tæn-cün</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>淋湿</rb><rt>ling-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>揩揩</rb><rt>kʽa-kʽa</rt><rb>燥</rb><rt>sao</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>走进来</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>嗅</rb><rt>hyüong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>弗歇</rb><rt>feh-hyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>*se-tsih*</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>同席</rb><rt>Dong-zih</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>？」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安稳稳</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. VIII.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>头遭子</rb><rt>deo-tsao-ts</rt><rb>犯了</rb><rt>væn-leh</rt><rb>恶鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>生病个</rb><rt>sang-bing-go</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>医好了</rb><rt>i-hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>*Mô-li-üô*</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>，）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>苦撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kwʽu-sah-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约亚拿</rb><rt>Iah-üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>苦撒</rb><rt>*Kwʽu-sah*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>家</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>苏撒拿</rb><rt>Su-sah-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>\[1\] 原书此处错印为 Mah-da-lah，后文均为 Meh-da-lah</sup>

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>扎开去</rb><rt>tsah-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>踔掉</rb><rt>nao-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>吃掉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>爆出</rb><rt>pao-cʽih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>枯落</rb><rt>kwʽu-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>湿气</rb><rt>sih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>缝里</rb><rt>vong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爆出来</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮煞</rb><rt>tsô-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>泥浴里</rb><rt>nyi-nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爆出来</rb><rt>pao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>籽</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>be</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>？」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>秘密</rb><rt>pi-mih</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>看弗出</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听听</rb><rt>tʽing-tʽing</rt><rb>听弗进</rb><rt>tʽing-feh-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>秧子</rb><rt>Iang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>*tih-lôh*</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>夺了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>跌落</rb><rt>*Tih-lôh*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒根头</rb><rt>m-keng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>暂时</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>试试个</rb><rt>s-s-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒退出</rb><rt>tao-tʽe-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>跌落</rb><rt>Tih-lôh</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>缝里</rb><rt>vong-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>今世个</rb><rt>kying-si-go</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遮住了</rb><rt>tsô-djü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒陶成</rb><rt>m-dao-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>跌落</rb><rt>*Tih-lôh*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>泥浴里</rb><rt>nyi-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>牢靠</rb><rt>lao-kʽao</rt><rb>把守</rb><rt>pô-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>耐耐心心</rb><rt>næ-næ-sing-sing</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>籽</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>点仔</rb><rt>tin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>罩东</rb><rt>tsao-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>眠床下</rb><rt>min-zông-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>灯台上</rb><rt>teng-dæ-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>走进来</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>S-ʽô</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>隐瞒</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>露出来</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>谨慎</rb><rt>kying-jing</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>听法</rb><rt>tʽing-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>夺了去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, nying-kô sön gyi. 或话，人家算其。（原注）</sup>

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>近身弗拢</rb><rt>gying-sing-feh-long</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>会着</rb><rt>we-djôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依勒</rb><rt>i-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>湖</rb><rt>wu</rt><rb>箇岸</rb><rt>keh-ngen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>驶</rb><rt>S</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>困熟了</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>怪风</rb><rt>kwa-fong</rt><rb>别落</rb><rt>bih-lôh</rt><rb>湖</rb><rt>wu</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>*min-teng*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浪</rb><rt>*lông*</rt><rb>拍进</rb><rt>*pʽah-tsing*</rt><rb>满</rb><rt>mun</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>式势</rb><rt>sih-shü</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>调觉</rb><rt>diao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>倒没了</rb><rt>tao-meh-de</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>浪</rb><rt>lông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>静落</rb><rt>zing-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>太平了</rb><rt>tʽa-bing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>讲来讲去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>！」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加大拉</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>对面</rb><rt>te-min</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>出身</rb><rt>cʽih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附勒</rb><rt>vu-leh</rt><rb>长久了</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>*deng*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>坟窠里</rb><rt>veng-kʽo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怀</rb><rt>wæ</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt><rb>嘶</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>磨难</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> （<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。）<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>硬弄</rb><rt>ngang-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>长久了</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>*se-tsih*</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>镣铐</rb><rt>liao-kʽao</rt><rb>锁仔</rb><rt>so-ts</rt><rb>关间</rb><rt>kwæn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>刑具</rb><rt>ying-gyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弄断</rb><rt>long-dön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>逼</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>营</rb><rt>Ying</rt></ruby>』。」<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*Kyü*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>呒底坑里</rb><rt>m-ti-kʽang-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>食</rb><rt>zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>队里</rb><rt>de-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应许了</rb><rt>ing-hyü-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>Kyü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>窜落</rb><rt>tsʽön-lôh</rt><rb>山岩</rb><rt>sæn-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>头落</rb><rt>deo-lôh</rt><rb>湖里</rb><rt>wu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>溢煞了</rb><rt>ih-sah-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔去</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>乡下</rb><rt>hyiang-ʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>穿的了</rb><rt>cʽün-tih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>清通了</rb><rt>tsʽing-tʽong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>医好个</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>加大拉</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>四凸乱圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>吓煞</rb><rt>hah-sah</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>转去了</rb><rt>cün-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做队</rb><rt>tso-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>39</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咸城里</rb><rt>ʽen-zing-li</rt><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>侍候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>间</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>睚鲁</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>独养</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拥勒去</rb><rt>üong-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>血漏</rb><rt>hyüih-leo</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>医生</rb><rt>i-sang</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>费完</rb><rt>fi-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>，<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>衦头</rb><rt>ken-den</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>血漏</rb><rt>hyüih-leo</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>止住</rb><rt>ts-djü</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>摸</rb><rt>moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>挨挨挤挤</rb><rt>a-a-tsi-tsi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>摸</rb><rt>moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？』」<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>摸</rb><rt>moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>瞒弗住</rb><rt>mun-feh-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>*kyiah-ʽô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>*gyi-zi*</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>Nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安稳稳</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>Wa</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>着力</rb><rt>dziah-lih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>娘子个</rb><rt>nyiang-ts-go</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>以外</rb><rt>yi-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>浡出</rb><rt>beh-cʽih</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>眼泪</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>困熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>冷笑</rb><rt>lang-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>娘子个</rb><rt>nyiang-ts-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>Nyiang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>还转来</rb><rt>wæn-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>爬起了</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>依点</rb><rt>i-tin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>吃一惊</rb><rt>kyʽih-ih-kying</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。

---

### Lk. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>有病个</rb><rt>yiu-bing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>棒</rb><rt>bông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>包袱</rb><rt>pao-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>备</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>两件</rb><rt>liang-gyin</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>谁拉</rb><rt>jü-lah</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>*deng*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>脚里个</rb><rt>kyiah-li-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>甩掉仔</rb><rt>hwah-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各到处</rb><rt>kôh-tao-cʽü</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>藩王</rb><rt>Væn-wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>疑惑不决</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出现</rb><rt>cʽih-yin</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>kwu-z-tsin</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>斩掉了</rb><rt>tsæn-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 32. 耶（稣）后（头）32（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt><rb>走转来</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>躲过</rb><rt>to-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯赛大</rb><rt>Pah-sæ-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>*siang-gying*</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>*bing*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>打笡</rb><rt>tang-tsʽia</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>村上</rb><rt>tsʽeng-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>乡下</rb><rt>hyiang-ʽô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>投宿</rb><rt>deo-soh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>两梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>一队</rb><rt>ih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>队打队</rb><rt>de-tang-de</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>两梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𤖼带开</rb><rt>pʽah-tæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>摆</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃过</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃饱了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>吃剩个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>收拾拢</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>*YIU*</rt><rb>一遭</rb><rt>ih-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>实惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>八</rb><rt>pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>别开</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>三顶</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>*gyi-zi*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>Tsing-hao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>盖落来</rb><rt>kæ-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>38</sup>
