<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附着</rb><rt>vu-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>一时里</rb><rt>ih-z-li</rt><rb>嘶起来</rb><rt>si-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活撞活颠</rb><rt>weh-djông-weh-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>黏馋</rb><rt>nyin-zæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>伤勒</rb><rt>sông-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>难得</rb><rt>næn-teh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走开</rb><rt>tseo-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>哎</rb><rt>ʽE</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>横绷勋流个</rb><rt>w̆ang-pang-hyüing-liu-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>*kyi-z*</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>Tsing-hao</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>活撞活颠</rb><rt>weh-djông-weh-tin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>Tsing-hao</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>44</sup> 「<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>𣥼勒</rb><rt>tsi-leh</rt><rb>耳朵里</rb><rt>ng-to-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>说话个</rb><rt>shih-wô-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>*ziang*</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话个</rb><rt>shih-wô-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心里个</rb><rt>sing-li-go</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt></ruby>，<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>*Ng*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>帮手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>升</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲定</rb><rt>kʽô-ding</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>打发</rb><rt>Tang-fah</rt><rb>差使</rb><rt>tsʽa-s</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>备办好</rb><rt>be-bæn-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>村上</rb><rt>*tsʽeng-zông*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招接</rb><rt>tsiao-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？」<sup>55</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>*nying-go*</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>*sing-ming*</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>跟勒去</rb><rt>keng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>58</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>狐狸</rb><rt>Wu-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>洞</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>窠</rb><rt>kʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>庉身之</rb><rt>deng-sing-ts</rt><rb>处</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>Keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>安葬好</rb><rt>en-tsông-hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>60</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>61</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>别</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>一别</rb><rt>ih-bih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>62</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>Siu</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>犁梢</rb><rt>li-sao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>朝后</rb><rt>dziao-ʽeo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>合着</rb><rt>ʽeh-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>七十个</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>两两</rb><rt>liang-liang</rt><rb>搭班</rb><rt>tah-pæn</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>Tseo-tsing</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>大街上</rb><rt>do-ka-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：11『<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>遭的个</rb><rt>tsao-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>掸落</rb><rt>tæn-lôh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇样</rb><rt>keh-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>近拢来了</rb><rt>gying-long-læ-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>12</sup> <sup>13</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哥拉汛</rb><rt>Ko-lah-sing</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯赛大</rb><rt>Pah-sæ-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>西顿</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>老早</rb><rt>lao-tsao</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>麻衣</rb><rt>mô-i</rt><rb>遭勒</rb><rt>tsao-leh</rt><rb>灰尘</rb><rt>hwe-dzing</rt><rb>回心转意了</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>西顿</u>个</rb><rt>Si-teng-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>举上</rb><rt>kyü-zông</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>推</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阴司里</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>听</rb><rt>Tʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七十个</rb><rt>tsʽih-jih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>走转来</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>服了</rb><rt>voh-lah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>闪电</rb><rt>sin-din</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>跌落来</rb><rt>tih-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>踏</rb><rt>dah</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>毒虫</rb><rt>doh-djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>压治</rb><rt>ah-djü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>势道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>服了</rb><rt>voh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>Cü-tsæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>遮住</rb><rt>tsô-djü</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>才能</rb><rt>dzæ-neng</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正是介</rb><rt>tsing-z-ka</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>样色</rb><rt>Yiang-seh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*Ah-tia*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>*m-nying*</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-sing kwu-kyün-li wô, Gyi ziu nyin-cün teng meng-du wô, “Yiang-seh t. y. t. 有星古卷里话，其就扭转等门徒话，「样色……（原注）</sup>

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>看个</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>*iao-siang*</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>听闻过</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ko</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>立出来</rb><rt>lih-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>律法里</rb><rt>Lih-fah-li</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>读法</rb><rt>doh-fah</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>心肠</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>精神</rb><rt>tsing-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全副</rb><rt>djün-fu</rt><rb>才情</rb><rt>dzæ-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>*tông-kæ*</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>*æ-sih*</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>回答勒</rb><rt>we-teh-leh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>错</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>剥</rb><rt>poh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打伤</rb><rt>tang-sông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>半死半活</rb><rt>pun-si-pun-weh</rt><rb>丢了去了</rb><rt>tiu-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>偶凑</rb><rt>Neo-tsʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>走落来</rb><rt>tseo-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>避过</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到勒</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>避过</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>倒是</rb><rt>Tao-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>过路个</rb><rt>ko-lu-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打伤</rb><rt>tang-sông</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>包好</rb><rt>pao-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>敷好</rb><rt>fu-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>挡上</rb><rt>tông-zông</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>骑仔</rb><rt>gyi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>下处里</rb><rt>ʽô-cʽü-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt><rb>二钿</rb><rt>nyi-din</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>下处里</rb><rt>ʽô-cʽü-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。』<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>好心</rb><rt>hao-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>走路个</rb><rt>tseo-lu-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心烦哜糟</rb><rt>sing-væn-tsi-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>供应</rb><rt>kong-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>管账</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>帮帮</rb><rt>pông-pông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>马大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>马大</rb><rt>Mô-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心烦哜糟</rb><rt>sing-væn-tsi-tsao</rt><rb>乱窜去</rb><rt>lön-tsʽön-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要紧个</rb><rt>iao-kying-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>行当</rb><rt>ʽông-tông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>拣着了</rb><rt>kæn-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. XI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祷告好</rb><rt>tao-kao-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：——

『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>*long-tsong*</rt><rb>尊敬</rb><rt>tseng-kying</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>逐日</rb><rt>djoh-nyih</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>Sô-diao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>朋友家</rb><rt>beng-yiu-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>半夜过</rb><rt>pun-yia-ko</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>借</rb><rt>tsia</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>过路</rb><rt>ko-lu</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>间</rb><rt>kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>摆过去</rb><rt>pa-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>Vong</rt><rb>吵</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>关了</rb><rt>kwæn-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>眠床</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>朋友家</rb><rt>beng-yiu-kô</rt><rb>情分</rb><rt>dzing-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>缚弗过</rb><rt>bo-feh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>用场</rb><rt>yüong-dziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>捣</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讨个</rb><rt>tʽao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>寻个</rb><rt>zing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>捣</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>*tʽao*</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一梗</rb><rt>ih-kwang</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>蛋</rb><rt>dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>毒虫</rb><rt>doh-djong</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>ʽo-hwông</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！」

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>哑</rb><rt>ô</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>Kyü</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲出</rb><rt>kông-cʽih</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>鬼王</rb><rt>kwe-wông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别西卜</rb><rt>Bih-si-poh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>还有得</rb><rt>Wa-yiu-teh</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>一国</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>分裂开</rb><rt>feng-lih-kʽæ</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>荒地</rb><rt>hwông-di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>败掉</rb><rt>ba-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>分裂开</rb><rt>feng-lih-kʽæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>立勒牢</rb><rt>lih-leh-lao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别西卜</rb><rt>Bih-si-poh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别西卜</rb><rt>Bih-si-poh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>指头</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>来东了</rb><rt>læ-tong-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>盔甲</rb><rt>kwʽe-kah</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>军器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>保牢</rb><rt>pao-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>打赢</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>夺</rb><rt>deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>倚恃个</rb><rt>i-z-go</rt><rb>盔甲</rb><rt>kwʽe-kah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>军器</rb><rt>kyüing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>收拢</rb><rt>siu-long</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>散掉</rb><rt>sæn-diao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>Ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荒旱</rb><rt>hwông-ʽen</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>走来走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>走转</rb><rt>tseo-cün</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>走出个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>扫</rb><rt>sao</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>装折好了</rb><rt>tsông-tsih-hao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>带领</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>结煞个</rb><rt>kyih-sah-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>坏了</rb><rt>w̆a-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>抬</rb><rt>Dæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喂</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>！」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>把守个</rb><rt>pô-siu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>Keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>挨拢来</rb><rt>a-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约拿</rb><rt>Iah-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>请圣</rb><rt>tsʽing-sing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>Nyi-nyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>南边个</rb><rt>Nen-pin-go</rt><rb>女皇</rb><rt>nyü-wông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>女皇</rb><rt>*nyü-wông*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>尽头</rb><rt>zing-deo</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所罗门</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所罗门</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>Nyi-nyi-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>尼尼微</rb><rt>*Nyi-nyi-vi*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约拿</rb><rt>Iah-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>点仔</rb><rt>tin-ts</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>暗藏</rb><rt>en-dzông</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>斗桶下</rb><rt>teo-dong-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>灯台上</rb><rt>teng-dæ-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>走进来</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>Ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>肌身个</rb><rt>kyi-sing-go</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浑身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>亮个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>*ngæn-tsing*</rt><rb>坏个</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浑身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>暗个</rb><rt>en-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>未免</rb><rt>vi-min</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>浑身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>亮</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正好像</rb><rt>tsing-hao-ziang</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>透亮个</rb><rt>tʽeo-liang-go</rt><rb>光</rb><rt>kwông</rt><rb>照着</rb><rt>tsiao-djôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>*siu*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>酒杯</rb><rt>tsiu-pe</rt><rb>盆子</rb><rt>beng-ts</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>强横</rb><rt>gyiang-wang</rt><rb>凶恶</rb><rt>hyüong-ôh</rt><rb>𣥼满了个</rb><rt>tsi-mun-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>Nyü-beng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>能使</rb><rt>Neng-s</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好事</rb><rt>hao-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>薄荷</rb><rt>bo-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>芸香</rb><rt>yüing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>菜</rb><rt>tsʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>*dæn-z*</rt><rb>荡样</rb><rt>dông-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇样</rb><rt>keh-yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>错落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>上位</rb><rt>zông-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>*hwun-hyi*</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>看弗出个</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-cʽih-go</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>走个</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>难挑个</rb><rt>næn-tʽiao-go</rt><rb>重担</rb><rt>djong-tæn</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>肩胛头</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>重担</rb><rt>djong-tæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一末</rb><rt>ih-meh</rt><rb>指头</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>bang-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去造</rb><rt>kyʽi-zao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>杀个</rb><rt>sah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>*ng-lah-go*</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>*tsu-tsong*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>聪明个</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*yiu-sing*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>50, 51</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>祭坛</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>大殿</rb><rt>da-din</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒迦利亚</u>个</rb><rt>Sah-kyüô-li-üô-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>讨还</rb><rt>tʽao-wæn</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真真</rb><rt>Tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>现在个</rb><rt>yin-dzæ-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>讨还</rb><rt>tʽao-wæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>知识个</rb><rt>cü-sih-go</rt><rb>钥匙</rb><rt>yiah-z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>夺勒去了</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走进个</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>*tseo-tsing-kyʽi*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>53</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>煞扣</rb><rt>sah-kʽeo</rt><rb>吵</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>激</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲出</rb><rt>kông-cʽih</rt><rb>算账</rb><rt>sön-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>单等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>亲口</rb><rt>tsʽing-kʽeo</rt><rb>讲出来</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>漏洞</rb><rt>leo-dong</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。

### Lk. XII.

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几万</rb><rt>kyi-væn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踔来踔去</rb><rt>nao-læ-nao-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>假好</rb><rt>kô-hao</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>暗藏</rb><rt>En-dzông</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>*m-yiu*</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>*ih-yiang*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>露出来</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>暗暗</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>亮亮</rb><rt>liang-liang</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>密弗洞风</rb><rt>mih-feh-dong-fong</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>张勒</rb><rt>tsiang-leh</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋顶</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>传开去</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>地狱里</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>五只</rb><rt>Ng-tsah</rt><rb>麻雀</rb><rt>mô-tsiang</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>二分</rb><rt>nyi-feng</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>买了</rb><rt>ma-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>忘记</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>头发</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>根根</rb><rt>keng-keng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>数好了</rb><rt>shü-hao-liao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>麻雀</rb><rt>mô-tsiang</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>赦弗掉个</rb><rt>sô-feh-diao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>愁</rb><rt>zeo</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>样式</rb><rt>yiang-shih</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>分剖</rb><rt>feng-pʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>遗落个</rb><rt>yi-lôh-go</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分分开</rb><rt>feng-feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>封</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>审</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>官司</rb><rt>kwun-s</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>谨防</rb><rt>kying-bông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>贪心</rb><rt>tʽen-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>*kô-kyi*</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>田地里个</rb><rt>din-di-li-go</rt><rb>年成</rb><rt>nyin-dzing</rt><rb>好勒紧</rb><rt>hao-leh-kying</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>出产</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt><rb>呒采</rb><rt>m-tsʽæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt></ruby>？』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>谷仓</rb><rt>koh-tsʽông</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>拆掉仔</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>大点</rb><rt>do-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>出产</rb><rt>cʽih-tsʽæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>囥</rb><rt>kʽông</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活灵</rb><rt>Weh-ling</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>货物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有伙</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>年数</rb><rt>nyin-su</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安心</rb><rt>en-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>。』<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>Nyü-beng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>备好间个</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>积聚</rb><rt>tsih-jü</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>*zeo-meng*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>性命</rb><rt>Sing-ming</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>穿着</rb><rt>cʽün-tsiah</rt><rb>贵重</rb><rt>*kwe-djong*</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup>
