<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>耽搁记</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kyi</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>男女个</rb><rt>nen-nyü-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>吃勒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-leh</rt><rb>醉醉</rb><rt>tse-tse</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>想弗到个</rb><rt>siang-feh-tao-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知个</rb><rt>teh-cü-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>Hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家个</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>防备</rb><rt>bông-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>板数</rb><rt>pæn-su</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>板数</rb><rt>pæn-su</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>打勒</rb><rt>tang-leh</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>*væn-pah*</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tsæn gyi nyüoh-tsiang: *ʽôh*, te-tsæn-kʽæ. 或话，斩其肉酱。或，对斩开。（原注）</sup>

<sup>49</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>着起来了</rb><rt>dziah-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>别样告</rb><rt>*bih-nyiang-kao*</rt></ruby>？<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>*si-li*</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>急法</rb><rt>kyih-fah</rt></ruby>！<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>造开</rb><rt>zao-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>造开</rb><rt>zao-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>阿婆</rb><rt>ah-bo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>新妇</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新妇</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿婆</rb><rt>ah-bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>造开</rb><rt>zao-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>西半边</rb><rt>si-pun-pin</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>马上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>落雨了</rb><rt>lôh-yü-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>雨</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落了</rb><rt>lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>*Kʽen-kyin*</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>南风</rb><rt>nen-fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>天家</rb><rt>Tʽin-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>热了</rb><rt>nyih-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽeo-deo*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>热了</rb><rt>nyih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看勒出</rb><rt>kʽen-leh-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>看弗出</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>57</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>*dao-li*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>辨</rb><rt>bin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>清通</rb><rt>tsʽing-tʽong</rt></ruby>？<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>半路里</rb><rt>pun-lu-li</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>上紧</rb><rt>zông-kying</rt><rb>打磨</rb><rt>tang-mo</rt><rb>脱出</rb><rt>tʽeh-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>皂吏</rb><rt>zao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>皂吏</rb><rt>zao-li</rt><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>煞脚个</rb><rt>Sah-kyiah-go</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>还完</rb><rt>wæn-wun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. XIII.

<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>*kyi-go*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt><rb>和拢了</rb><rt>ʽo-long-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>*ʽo-se*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十八个</rb><rt>jih-pah-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西罗亚</rb><rt>Si-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>高楼</rb><rt>kao-leo</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>压煞个</rb><rt>ah-sah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>住</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>格外</rb><rt>kah-nga</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, kyʽin tsa. 本文字眼，欠债。（原注）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>葡萄园里</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün-li</rt><rb>种间</rb><rt>cong-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寻寻</rb><rt>*zing-zing*</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>园头</rb><rt>yün-deo</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇株</rb><rt>Keh-cü</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寻寻</rb><rt>*zing-zing*</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斫掉</rb><rt>tsôh-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讨力</rb><rt>tʽao-lih</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>园头</rb><rt>Yün-deo</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今年</rb><rt>kying-nyin</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>团团</rb><rt>dön-dön</rt><rb>掘</rb><rt>gyüih</rt><rb>一掘</rb><rt>ih-gyüih</rt><rb>壅壅</rb><rt>üong-üong</rt><rb>粪</rb><rt>feng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*ziu*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斫</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>安息</rb><rt>EN-SIH</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>风病</rb><rt>fong-bing</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>十八</rb><rt>jih-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腰骨</rb><rt>iao-kweh</rt><rb>藃拢</rb><rt>ho-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>直弗起</rb><rt>dzih-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴拢来</rb><rt>eo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>脱掉了</rb><rt>tʽeh-diao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>*nyü-nying*</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>直起来了</rb><rt>dzih-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>管</rb><rt>Kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>*loh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>假好人</rb><rt>Kô-hao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一般</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>圈里</rb><rt>gyin-li</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牵出</rb><rt>kyʽin-cʽih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>况且</rb><rt>Hwông-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>缚住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>十八</rb><rt>jih-pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>惶恐了</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做出来个</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>有伙</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>光明正大</rb><rt>kwông-ming-tsing-da</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>欢喜了</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>Kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>驮仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>花园里</rb><rt>hwô-yün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>长起来</rb><rt>dziang-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>成勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>一株</rb><rt>ih-cü</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>桠枝里</rb><rt>ô-ts-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>Kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>囥勒</rb><rt>kʽông-leh</rt><rb>三斗</rb><rt>sæn-teo</rt><rb>面粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>发酵</rb><rt>fah-kao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>顺埭</rb><rt>jing-da</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>撑进</rb><rt>tsʽang-tsing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>狭窄个</rb><rt>ʽah-tsah-go</rt><rb>门里</rb><rt>meng-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>关好</rb><rt>kwæn-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>开开</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>』——<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>吃过了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko-de</rt><rb>喝过了</rb><rt>hah-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大街上</rb><rt>do-ka-zông</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非礼</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>走开去</rb><rt>tseo-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>以撒</rb><rt>Yi-sah</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神国</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>赶出东</rb><rt>ken-cʽih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>啼哭个</rb><rt>di-kʽoh-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>东边</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>西边</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>南边</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt><rb>北边</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神国里</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>在后个</rb><rt>dzæ-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>在前个</rb><rt>dzæ-zin-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落后</rb><rt>lôh-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>别</rb><rt>bih</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>狐狸</rb><rt>wu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病痛</rb><rt>bing-tʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>全备了</rb><rt>djün-be-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>明朝</rb><rt>ming-tsiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>后日</rb><rt>ʽeo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走个</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>弄煞个</rb><rt>long-sah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>！<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>！<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞</rb><rt>kʽang-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>回数</rb><rt>we-su</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>聚</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢来</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>鸡娘</rb><rt>kyi-nyiang</rt><rb>翼梢</rb><rt>yiah-sao</rt><rb>底下</rb><rt>ti-ʽô</rt><rb>捂</rb><rt>*u*</rt><rb>小鸡</rb><rt>siao-kyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby>！<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>变勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>空地</rb><rt>kʽong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是应该</rb><rt>z-ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝赞个</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。」
