### Lk. XIV.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>头脑拉</rb><rt>deo-nao-lah</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>偷伴</rb><rt>tʽeo-bun</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>东</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>当</rb><rt>Tông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>臌胀</rb><rt>kwu-tsiang</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>En-sih-nyih</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>好使得</rb><rt>hao-s-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>医好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>一头</rb><rt>ih-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>地坑里</rb><rt>di-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>撩</rb><rt>liao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上来</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>应弗来</rb><rt>ing-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>请间个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拣</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>头位</rb><rt>deo-we</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>头位</rb><rt>deo-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>请间个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>尊重个</rb><rt>tseng-djong-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>让</rb><rt>nyiang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>惶恐相</rb><rt>wông-kʽong-siang</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>Tsih-yiu</rt><rb>请去个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>朋友</rb><rt>Beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>*zo-ts*</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>坐客</rb><rt>zo-kʽah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>体面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>升</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>备</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>昼饭</rb><rt>tsiu-væn</rt><rb>备</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>夜饭</rb><rt>yia-væn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>得着了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>下饭</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>穷苦</rb><rt>gyüong-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>残疾</rb><rt>dzæn-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wæn</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>礼</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>坐客</rb><rt>Zo-kʽah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>神国里</rb><rt>Jing-koh-li</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>办了</rb><rt>bæn-leh</rt><rb>一席</rb><rt>ih-zih</rt><rb>大酒</rb><rt>do-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>坐席个</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>请间个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>备好了</rb><rt>be-hao-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>*sing-siang*</rt><rb>回复了</rb><rt>we-foh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>头一个</rb><rt>Deo-ih-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一畈</rb><rt>ih-pæn</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>买间</rb><rt>ma-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>务必子</rb><rt>vu-pih-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看过个</rb><rt>kʽen-ko-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>原谅</rb><rt>nyün-liang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十头</rb><rt>jih-deo</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>买间</rb><rt>ma-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>试试看</rb><rt>s-s-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>原谅</rb><rt>nyün-liang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>刚刚</rb><rt>*kông-kông*</rt><rb>好日过</rb><rt>hao-nyih-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>走归</rb><rt>tseo-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>Kwʽa</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>街街弄弄</rb><rt>ka-ka-long-long</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>穷苦</rb><rt>gyüong-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>残疾</rb><rt>dzæn-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>做好了</rb><rt>tso-hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>空个</rb><rt>kʽong-go</rt><rb>坐头</rb><rt>zo-deo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>Cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>大路上</rb><rt>do-lu-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>枪笆沿</rb><rt>tsʽiang-pô-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>*læ-kæn-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>进来</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>坐满</rb><rt>zo-mun</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>请间个</rb><rt>tsʽing-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>下饭个</rb><rt>ʽô-væn-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>尝尝看</rb><rt>zông-zông-kʽen</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>看轻</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>揹</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>高楼</rb><rt>kao-leo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt><rb>派</rb><rt>pʽa</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>费用</rb><rt>fi-yüong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>*dong-din*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt><rb>够了</rb><rt>keo-de</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>Sæn-leh</rt><rb>地盘</rb><rt>di-bun</rt><rb>安好仔</rb><rt>en-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>完工弗来</rb><rt>wun-kong-feh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>*ih*</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>*kʽen*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>30</sup> 『<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>造造</rb><rt>zao-zao</rt><rb>高楼</rb><rt>*kao-leo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>*tsih-yiu*</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>完工</rb><rt>wun-kong</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt><rb>商量好</rb><rt>siang-liang-hao</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一万</rb><rt>ih-væn</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抵敌</rb><rt>ti-dih</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>二万</rb><rt>nyi-væn</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>打勒</rb><rt>tang-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？』<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>趁</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求和</rb><rt>gyiu-ʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>Yin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咸个</rb><rt>ʽæn-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽæn</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>纯</rb><rt>*Jing*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>壅</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>呒做</rb><rt>m-tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和勒</rb><rt>ʽo-leh</rt><rb>肥粪</rb><rt>bi-feng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒做</rb><rt>m-tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>倒勒</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. XV.

<ruby><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>SIU-ZIN-LIANG</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>收留</rb><rt>siu-liu</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百只</rb><rt>ih-pah-tsah</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>弗见了</rb><rt>feh-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>九十</rb><rt>kyiu-jih</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>弗见个</rb><rt>feh-kyin-go</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>djôh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>寻着仔</rb><rt>Zing-djôh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>肩胛头</rb><rt>kyin-kah-deo</rt><rb>揹仔</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇只</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>弗见个</rb><rt>feh-kyin-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>欢喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>九十</rb><rt>kyiu-jih</rt><rb>九</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>*yü-kô*</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>*hwun-hyi*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽOh-tsia</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十块</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>弗见了</rb><rt>feh-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>点勒</rb><rt>tin-leh</rt><rb>蜡烛</rb><rt>lah-coh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扫扫</rb><rt>sao-sao</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仔仔细细</rb><rt>ts-ts-si-si</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>djôh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>寻着仔</rb><rt>Zing-djôh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>女人队</rb><rt>nyü-nying-de</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>ling-sô-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇块</rb><rt>Keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>弗见个</rb><rt>feh-kyin-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>欢喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。』<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Dông-deo* *wô* jih-kwʽe nying-ts, *me-kwʽe* *ih-din* *djong*. 荡头话十块银子，每块一钿重。（原注）</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爹爹</rb><rt>Tia-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>仔</rb><rt>ts</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*Ah-tia*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>分𤖼开</rb><rt>feng-pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>呒几</rb><rt>m-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>*kô-kyi*</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>收拾拢</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>出门</rb><rt>cʽih-meng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>游戏浪荡</rb><rt>yiu-hyi-lông-dông</rt><rb>花掉</rb><rt>hwô-diao</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>费完了</rb><rt>fi-wun-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒吃用</rb><rt>m-kyʽüoh-yüong</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇份</rb><rt>*keh-veng*</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>田头</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>担</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>饭</rb><rt>*væn*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>饱饱</rb><rt>pao-pao</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>一记</rb><rt>Ih-kyi</rt><rb>醒头到</rb><rt>sing-deo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>箇打</rb><rt>keh-tang</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>饭粮</rb><rt>væn-liang</rt><rb>吃过</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>饿煞</rb><rt>ngo-sah</rt></ruby>！<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>爹爹</rb><rt>Tia-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>看待</rb><rt>kʽen-dæ</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>Lu</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>有伙</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>看见了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奔上去</rb><rt>peng-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捼勒</rb><rt>o-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嗅嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>爹爹</rb><rt>Tia-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。』<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇件</rb><rt>Keh-gyin</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>长衫</rb><rt>dziang-sæn</rt><rb>驮出来</rb><rt>do-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>戒指</rb><rt>ka-ts</rt><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>脚里</rb><rt>kyiah-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇只</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>壮壮个</rb><rt>công-công-go</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt><rb>牵出来</rb><rt>kyʽin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>杀仔</rb><rt>sah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗见仔</rb><rt>feh-kyin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>快活</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>田头</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>弹唱</rb><rt>dæn-tsʽông</rt><rb>跳舞个</rb><rt>tʽiao-vu-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问问</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>佣人</rb><rt>Yüong-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>进来</rb><rt>tsing-læ</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒毛病</rb><rt>m-mao-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>壮壮</rb><rt>công-công</rt><rb>箇只</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt><rb>杀掉了</rb><rt>sah-diao-de</rt></ruby>。』<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>*Do*</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>年数</rb><rt>nyin-su</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>从来</rb><rt>dzong-læ</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>朋友家</rb><rt>beng-yiu-kô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>快活记</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh-kyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>吃没了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-mah-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>壮壮</rb><rt>công-công</rt><rb>小牛</rb><rt>siao-ngeo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*Ah-lah*</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>快活记</rb><rt>kwʽa-weh-kyi</rt><rb>欢喜相</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-siang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗见仔</rb><rt>feh-kyin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。』」

---

### Lk. XVI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>一份</rb><rt>Ih-veng</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>把账</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>告诉</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>良士</rb><rt>lang-z</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>把账</rb><rt>Pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>费掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt></ruby>。』<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>良士</rb><rt>*Lang-z*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>把账</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把账</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>做弗来了</rb><rt>tso-feh-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>经手个</rb><rt>kying-siu-go</rt><rb>账目</rb><rt>tsiang-moh</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>把账</rb><rt>Pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把账个</rb><rt>pô-tsiang-go</rt><rb>缺分</rb><rt>kyʽüih-veng</rt><rb>良士</rb><rt>lang-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>革掉</rb><rt>kah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄法</rb><rt>long-fah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>耕</rb><rt>kang</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呒气力</rb><rt>m-kyʽi-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>讨饭</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>晓得了</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>把账</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>缺分</rb><rt>kyʽüih-veng</rt><rb>革掉</rb><rt>kah-diao</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>良士</rb><rt>lang-z</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>讴拢来</rb><rt>eo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>头一个</rb><rt>deo-ih-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>良士</rb><rt>lang-z</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt></ruby>？』<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>欠</rb><rt>*Kyʽin*</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百篓</rb><rt>ih-pah-leo</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>把账</rb><rt>*Pô-tsiang*</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>*sin-sang*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>票子</rb><rt>pʽiao-ts</rt><rb>驮仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>五十篓</rb><rt>ng-jih-*leo*</rt></ruby>。』<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>麦</rb><rt>Mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百石</rb><rt>ih-pah-zah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。』<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>票子</rb><rt>pʽiao-ts</rt><rb>驮仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>八十石</rb><rt>pah-jih-*zah*</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>良士</rb><rt>Lang-z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>把账</rb><rt>pô-tsiang</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>巧</rb><rt>kyʽiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>时世个</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>光明个</rb><rt>kwông-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>巧</rb><rt>kyʽiao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-tʽa nying ih-zah, Ning-po ʽoh-ts s-zah-to. <u>犹太</u>人一石，宁波斛子四石多。（原注）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>用场</rb><rt>*yüong-dziang*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>结交</rb><rt>kyih-kyiao</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>正个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>*dzæ-veh*</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>别人个</rb><rt>bih-nying-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>M-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看轻</rb><rt>kʽen-kyʽing</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Peng-veng* *z-ngæn*, Ma-mo-na. 本文字眼，玛摩那。（原注）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>贪财个</rb><rt>tʽen-dzæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>冷笑</rb><rt>lang-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>郑重个</rb><rt>dzing-djong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>秽污个</rb><rt>w̆e-u-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>传落</rb><rt>djün-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>从此以后</rb><rt>dzong-tsʽ-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>传开去</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>个个</rb><rt>ko-ko</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>力</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>撑进去</rb><rt>tsʽang-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一点</rb><rt>ih-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>离掉个</rb><rt>li-diao-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>宝蓝</rb><rt>pao-læn</rt><rb>缎</rb><rt>dön</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>细</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>吃勒</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-leh</rt><rb>奢华</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>浑身</rb><rt>*weng-sing*</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>疮</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有佬个</rb><rt>yiu-lao-go</rt><rb>桌凳</rb><rt>coh-teng</rt><rb>跌落个</rb><rt>tih-lôh-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>黄狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>舔舔</rb><rt>tʽin-tʽin</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>疮</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>*ʽEo-deo*</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>抱勒去</rb><rt>bao-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安葬好</rb><rt>en-tsông-hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>阴司里</rb><rt>ing-s-li</rt><rb>吃苦</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怀怀</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>Tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>指头尖</rb><rt>ts-deo-tsin</rt><rb>揾点</rb><rt>w̆eng-tin</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>舌头</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>冷阴记</rb><rt>lang-ing-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>火焰</rb><rt>ho-yin</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>苦弗过</rb><rt>kwʽu-feh-ko</rt></ruby>。』<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>儿孙</rb><rt>Ng-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>活前</rb><rt>weh-zin</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>受过</rb><rt>ziu-ko</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>难头</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>受过</rb><rt>*ziu-ko*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>安稳</rb><rt>en-w̆eng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>吃苦了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽu-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>隔开</rb><rt>kah-kʽæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>坑</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>造成了</rb><rt>zao-dzing-liao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>Lah-sah-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>*eo*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拉撒路</rb><rt>*Lah-sah-lu*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>劝劝</rb><rt>kyʽün-kyʽün</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>吃苦个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kwʽu-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>*kyʽün*</rt></ruby>。』<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>Tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>覅</rb><rt>fæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>*Üô-pah-lah-hen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>劝</rb><rt>kyʽün</rt></ruby>。』」

---

### Lk. XVII.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>带累</rb><rt>Ta-li</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>弄出来</rb><rt>long-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>带累</rb><rt>Ta-li</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>磨上层</rb><rt>mo-zông-zeng</rt><rb>挂</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>*ying-vah*</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦回</rb><rt>sô-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>回心转意了</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>饶</rb><rt>nyiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>一日</rb><rt>Ih-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七遭</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsao</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>七遭</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsao</rt><rb>走转来</rb><rt>tseo-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>回心转意了</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>却勒</rb><rt>kyʽiah-leh</rt><rb>饶</rb><rt>nyiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>*Gyiu*</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>*Ng*</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇株</rb><rt>keh-cü</rt><rb>枫树</rb><rt>fong-jü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>根头</rb><rt>Keng-deo</rt><rb>拔起</rb><rt>bah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>种勒</rb><rt>cong-leh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>耕</rb><rt>kang</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>田头</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>马上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>？』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>下饭</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>备好</rb><rt>be-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>喝个</rb><rt>hah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>缴身带</rb><rt>kyiao-sing-ta</rt><rb>系仔</rb><rt>kyi-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>侍候东</rb><rt>z-ʽeo-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>？』<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>过谢</rb><rt>ko-zia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>𣍐个</rb><rt>ve-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>吩咐个</rb><rt>feng-fu-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做好</rb><rt>tso-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>*zi*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒用个</rb><rt>m-yüong-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做过个</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>门份</rb><rt>meng-veng</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交界</rb><rt>kao-ka</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>Tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>碰见</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin</rt><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>大麻风</rb><rt>da-mô-fong</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>干净了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒走转</rb><rt>tao-tseo-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>和十个</rb><rt>ʽo-jih-go</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>干净了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>九个头</rb><rt>kyiu-go-den</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>Tsih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>外国人</rb><rt>nga-koh-nying</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>FAH-LI-SÆ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看勒见个</rb><rt>kʽen-leh-kyin-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>！』<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>！』<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, feh-z tsiao-liao-tong hao kʽen-kyin-go. 或话，弗是照料东好看见个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, læ ng-lah sing-li. 有得翻，来尔拉心里。（原注）</sup>

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>Tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>Ng-ts-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>闪电</rb><rt>sin-din</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>亮过</rb><rt>liang-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>箇代</rb><rt>keh-dæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亚</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候个</rb><rt>z-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亚</rb><rt>*Nô-üô*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>娶进</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>挪亚</rb><rt>Nô-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>方船</rb><rt>fông-jün</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洪水泛涨</rb><rt>ʽong-shü-fæn-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>罗得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>喝个</rb><rt>hah-go</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>买进个</rb><rt>ma-tsing-go</rt><rb>买进</rb><rt>ma-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>卖出个</rb><rt>ma-cʽih-go</rt><rb>卖出</rb><rt>ma-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>种田个</rb><rt>cong-din-go</rt><rb>种</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>田</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>起屋个</rb><rt>kyʽi-oh-go</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>oh</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>罗得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多玛</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>落落来</rb><rt>lôh-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋顶</rb><rt>oh-teng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手用家生</rb><rt>siu-yüong-kô-sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>田头</rb><rt>din-deo</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>走归来</rb><rt>tseo-kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>罗得</rb><rt>Lo-teh</rt><rb>老𭑸个</rb><rt>lao-nyüing-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>记得记</rb><rt>kyi-teh-kyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>*kʽeng*</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>眠床</rb><rt>min-zông</rt><rb>困的</rb><rt>*kwʽeng-tih*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>收勒去</rb><rt>siu-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>剩落东</rb><rt>dzing-lôh-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <sup>\[1\] Keh 36 tsih, yiu-sing iao-kying kwu-kyün-li m̆-teh-go. 箇 36 节，有星要紧古卷里呒得个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>*yiu*</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老鹰</rb><rt>lao-ing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>。」
