### Lk. XVIII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>懈怠</rb><rt>yiæ-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>理刑厅</rb><rt>li-ying-tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>愀睬</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>*Gyiu*</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>*ng*</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>冤</rb><rt>ün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>冤家个</rb><rt>ün-kô-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。』<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>官</rb><rt>*Kwun*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>愀睬</rb><rt>tsʽiu-tsʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>只管</rb><rt>*coh-kwun*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>啰唆</rb><rt>l̆o-so</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>吵闹</rb><rt>tsʽao-nao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>理刑厅</rb><rt>li-ying-tʽing</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>拣择个</rb><rt>kæn-dzeh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>夜夜</rb><rt>yia-yia</rt><rb>怀怀</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>冤</rb><rt>ün</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>*kʽen-kyin*</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>弗起</rb><rt>feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>：<sup>10</sup> 「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>谢谢</rb><rt>zia-zia</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>抢夺</rb><rt>tsʽiang-deh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>公道</rb><rt>kong-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>Me</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>礼拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两遭</rb><rt>liang-tsao</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十股</rb><rt>jih-kwu</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>哦</rb><rt>ô</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>犯罪个</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>收钱粮</rb><rt>siu-zin-liang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>低</rb><rt>ti</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>升</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*YIU-SING*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢来</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神国</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>问问</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>谎告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>』 」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>顾着个</rb><rt>kwu-djôh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>统</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>！<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>骆驼</rb><rt>Loh-do</rt><rb>穿过</rb><rt>cʽün-ko</rt><rb>针眼</rb><rt>tsing-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>听懂个</rb><rt>tʽing-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>做弗来</rb><rt>tso-feh-læ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>弃掉仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今世</rb><rt>kying-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>倍数</rb><rt>be-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>下世</rb><rt>ʽô-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>*teh-djôh*</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>应验</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>戏弄</rb><rt>hyi-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<sup>33</sup> </sup><ruby><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>生意</rb><rt>sang-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt></ruby>。』<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>贵重个</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>国王个</rb><rt>koh-wông-go</rt><rb>职份</rb><rt>tsih-veng</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>kyü-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>多少</rb><rt>to-siao</rt><rb>赚头</rb><rt>dzæn-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>头一个</rb><rt>Deo-ih-go</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十块</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>赚头</rb><rt>dzæn-deo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>*Cü-nying-kô*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>好好</rb><rt>Hao-hao</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>忠心</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>县份</rb><rt>yün-veng</rt></ruby>。』<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第二个</rb><rt>Di-nyi-go</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五块</rb><rt>ng-kwʽe</rt><rb>赚头</rb><rt>dzæn-deo</rt></ruby>。』<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>县份</rb><rt>yün-veng</rt></ruby>。』<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kʽwe</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>包勒</rb><rt>pao-leh</rt><rb>手巾里</rb><rt>siu-kying-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>安间个</rb><rt>en-kæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>驮归</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>种间个</rb><rt>cong-kæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>。』<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>*Cü-nying-kô*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>口供</rb><rt>kʽeo-kong</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>*Kyi-jün*</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>安间个</rb><rt>en-kæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>驮归</rb><rt>do-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>种间个</rb><rt>cong-kæn-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>存勒</rb><rt>dzeng-leh</rt><rb>钱店里</rb><rt>dzin-tin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>利钿</rb><rt>li-din</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收归</rb><rt>siu-kyü</rt></ruby>？』<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>*Cü-nying-kô*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十块头</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe-den</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十块</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>！』<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>主人家</rb><rt>*Cü-nying-kô*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>揸勒</rb><rt>cô-leh</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>\[1\] Me-kwʽe da-iah jih-to-liang djong. 每块大约十多两重。（原注）</sup>

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>KWʽA</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯法基</rb><rt>Pah-fah-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>箇座</rb><rt>*keh-zo*</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>*sæn*</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>30</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对头个</rb><rt>te-deo-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>桩间</rb><rt>công-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从来</rb><rt>dzong-læ</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>骑过</rb><rt>gyi-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>解仔</rb><rt>ka-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牵勒来</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>*li-ts*</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>差去个</rb><rt>Tsʽa-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*keh-deo*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>居</rb><rt>ke</rt><rb>箇匹</rb><rt>keh-pʽih</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>牵勒</rb><rt>kyʽin-leh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>驴子</rb><rt>li-ts</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挡勒</rb><rt>tông-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>骑上去</rb><rt>gyi-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>铺</rb><rt>pʽu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路里</rb><rt>lu-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>*Yiæ-lu-sah-leng*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>看见过个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有伙</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>38</sup> 「<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>Kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>祝赞个</rb><rt>coh-tsæn-go</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>但愿</rb><rt>*dæn-nyün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>太平</rb><rt>tʽa-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>但愿</rb><rt>*dæn-nyün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>！」<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>Di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>竟是介</rb><rt>kying-z-ka</rt><rb>打打伤</rb><rt>tang-tang-sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>居园个</rb><rt>Ke-yün-go</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>*keh-tsao*</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>值钿个</rb><rt>dzih-din-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>种田人</rb><rt>Cong-din-nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接着</rb><rt>tsih-djôh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>杀勒</rb><rt>sah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>产业</rb><rt>tsʽæn-nyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。』<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>推</rb><rt>tʽe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>园外</rb><rt>yün-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杀掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>居园个</rb><rt>ke-yün-go</rt><rb>业主</rb><rt>nyih-cü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>？<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>种田人</rb><rt>cong-din-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>葡萄园</rb><rt>bu-dao-yün</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>起屋个</rb><rt>Kyʽi-oh-go</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>屋</rb><rt>*oh*</rt><rb>角落头</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt><rb>头一块</rb><rt>deo-ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>』 <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头上</rb><rt>zah-deo-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>跌糊</rb><rt>tih-wu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇块</rb><rt>*keh-kwʽe*</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>*zah-deo*</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>压勒</rb><rt>ah-leh</rt><rb>粉碎</rb><rt>feng-se</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>趁此机会</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽ-kyi-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>探听</rb><rt>tʽen-tʽing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假充</rb><rt>kô-cʽong</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>讹头</rb><rt>ngo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>总督个</rb><rt>tsong-toh-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>直</rb><rt>dzih</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>准</rb><rt>cing</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>实实惠惠</rb><rt>jih-jih-we-we</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>投</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>钱粮</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>奸计</rb><rt>kæn-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>十块</rb><rt>jih-kwʽe</rt><rb>番饼</rb><rt>fæn-ping</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>头子</rb><rt>Deo-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>国号</rb><rt>koh-ʽao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁侬个</rb><rt>jü-noh-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讹头</rb><rt>ngo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗响了</rb><rt>feh-hyiang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答个</rb><rt>we-teh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Keh-kwʽe* *fæn-ping* *dzih* *ih-din* *nying-ts*. 该块番饼值一钿银子。（原注）</sup>

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒土该</rb><rt>Sah-tʽu-kæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>剩勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿弟</rb><rt>ah-di</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿哥</rb><rt>ah-ko</rt><rb>传宗接代</rb><rt>djün-cong-tsih-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>*ih-veng*</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>抬了</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>抬了</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>Di-sæn</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>和七个</rb><rt>ʽo-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>交落</rb><rt>gao-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>Yin-dzæ</rt><rb>时世个</rb><rt>z-shü-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>娶进</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>时世</rb><rt>z-shü</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>娶进</rb><rt>cʽü-tsing</rt><rb>嫁出</rb><rt>kô-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>讲到</rb><rt>kông-tao</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>Tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>箇篇</rb><rt>keh-pʽin</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>明明</rb><rt>ming-ming</rt><rb>指点过</rb><rt>ts-tin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>以撒</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-sah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>死人个</rb><rt>si-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活人个</rb><rt>weh-nying-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, gyi-lah long-tsong we-leh Jing-ming weh-go: *kʽen* Lm. 6. 10, 11. 或话，其拉拢总为了神明活个。看 Lm. 6. 10, 11.（原注）</sup>

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>以后</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣诗里</rb><rt>Sing-s-li</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>仇敌</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>踏脚凳</rb><rt>dah-kyiah-teng</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>*kyi-jün*</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>听东</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>46</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>谨防</rb><rt>kying-bông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>长长</rb><rt>dziang-dziang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>走来走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>上位</rb><rt>zông-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒筵上</rb><rt>tsiu-yin-zông</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>头位</rb><rt>deo-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>吞吃</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸个</rb><rt>lao-nyüing-go</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>假戏头</rb><rt>kô-hyi-deo</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>漫长</rb><rt>mæn-dziang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受罪</rb><rt>ziu-ze</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. XXI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>钱库里</rb><rt>dzin-kwʽu-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>穷</rb><rt>gyüong</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-ten</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>实惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>穷穷</rb><rt>gyüong-gyüong</rt><rb>孤孀</rb><rt>kwu-sông</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>丢的个</rb><rt>tiu-tih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>多头</rb><rt>to-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>礼物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>丢点的</rb><rt>tiu-tin-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>缺利利</rb><rt>kyʽüih-li-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>丢进的了</rb><rt>tiu-tsing-tih-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Keh* *liang* *dong-din* *dzih* *ih-li* *nying-ts*: *kʽen* Mk. 12. 42. 箇两铜钿值一厘银子。看 Mk. 12. 42.（原注）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>助拢来个</rb><rt>dzu-long-læ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>装饰东</rb><rt>tsông-sih-tong</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>看个</rb><rt>kʽen-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>到其</rb><rt>tao-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连只</rb><rt>*lin-tsih*</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>剩落</rb><rt>dzing-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>上顶</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>带掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>冒勒</rb><rt>mao-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。』<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>缭乱个</rb><rt>liao-lön-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>马上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>*ziu*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, yi *wô*, Z-ʽeo gying-de. Kʽen Mt. 3. 2: 4. 17. 或话，又话，时候近了。看 Mt. 3. 2: 4. 17.（原注）</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>时疫</rb><rt>z-yüoh</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>怕怕</rb><rt>pʽô-pʽô</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>*feh-zing*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赶勒害</rb><rt>ken-leh-ʽæ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挈</rb><rt>kyʽih</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>总督个</rb><rt>tsong-toh-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>过去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>今世个</rb><rt>kying-si-go</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>呆顿顿</rb><rt>ngæ-teng-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>突然</rb><rt>deh-jün</rt><rb>临着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>弶</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>鸟兽</rb><rt>tiao-siu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>*ih*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>普天地下</rb><rt>pʽu-tʽin-di-ʽô</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>庉的个</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罩进</rb><rt>tsao-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>苏醒</rb><rt>su-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>避掉</rb><rt>bi-diao</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>每日</rb><rt>me-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每夜</rb><rt>me-yia</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>*sæn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>宿夜</rb><rt>soh-yia</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>Cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。
