### Lk. XXII.

<ruby><rb>无酵饼个</rb><rt>VU-KAO-PING-GO</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>十二个</rb><rt>Jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加略</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>入进</rb><rt>jih-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>侍卫</rb><rt>z-we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲开</rb><rt>kông-kʽæ</rt><rb>给</rb><rt>coh</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>*Yiu-da*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应承</rb><rt>ing-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>闹事</rb><rt>nao-z</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>机会</rb><rt>kyi-we</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, cong-nying m-neh læ-tong go kyi-we. 或话，众人呒得来东个机会。（原注）</sup>

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>无酵节</rb><rt>VU-KAO-TSIH</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>备办</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt></ruby>？」<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一瓶</rb><rt>ih-bing</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>挈的</rb><rt>kyʽih-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走进个</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇份</rb><rt>keh-veng</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>箇间</rb><rt>keh-kæn</rt><rb>客房</rb><rt>kʽah-vông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？』<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>摆设好了</rb><rt>pa-shih-hao-liao</rt><rb>一间</rb><rt>ih-kæn</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>楼顶</rb><rt>leo-teng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>备好间</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt><rb>备好间</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>坐席</rb><rt>zo-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐席</rb><rt>*zo-zih*</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>企慕</rb><rt>kyʽi-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神国</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>接受了</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-leh</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝谢好</rb><rt>coh-zia-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分带开</rb><rt>feng-tæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝谢仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>舍出个</rb><rt>sô-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>*Yü-yüih*</rt><rb>吃好仔</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>*do-leh*</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>簇崭新个</rb><rt>tsʽoh-tsæn-sing-go</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>设立个</rb><rt>*shih-lih-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>流出来个</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>桌凳上</rb><rt>coh-teng-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>原是</rb><rt>nyün-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>定好了个</rb><rt>ding-hao-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>祸祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>问来问去</rb><rt>meng-læ-meng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>别国个</rb><rt>bih-koh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>压实</rb><rt>ah-jih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>执掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恩人</rb><rt>eng-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>当值个</rb><rt>tông-dzih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>坐席个</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>当值个</rb><rt>tông-dzih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>坐席个</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>当值个</rb><rt>tông-dzih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>遭难个</rb><rt>tsao-næn-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>约好</rb><rt>iah-hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>约</rb><rt>iah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>国里</rb><rt>koh-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做桌</rb><rt>tso-coh</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>喝喝</rb><rt>hah-hah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位里</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>*gyün-ping*</rt><rb>讨过了</rb><rt>tʽao-ko-de</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>簸拢</rb><rt>po-long</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>麦</rb><rt>mah</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔侬</rb><rt>ng-noh</rt><rb>求过了</rb><rt>gyiu-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>复转来</rb><rt>voh-cün-læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>坚固</rb><rt>kyin-kwu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>兄弟个</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>落监</rb><rt>lôh-kæn</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>情愿个</rb><rt>dzing-nyün-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末仔</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鸡</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>赏满</rb><rt>sông-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>包袱</rb><rt>pao-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>缺少</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒告</rb><rt>M-kao</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>*kyʽüih*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>有勒</rb><rt>yiu-leh</rt><rb>赏满</rb><rt>sông-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>带勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有勒</rb><rt>*yiu-leh*</rt><rb>包袱</rb><rt>pao-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>一把</rb><rt>ih-pô</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>算进</rb><rt>sön-tsing</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>』 <ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>应着</rb><rt>ing-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两把</rb><rt>liang-pô</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>够了</rb><rt>Keo-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出仔</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>闲常</rb><rt>ʽæn-djông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞起</rb><rt>kʽang-kyʽi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>远</rb><rt>*yün*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>脚䯊头</rb><rt>kyiah-kʽo-deo</rt><rb>跪落</rb><rt>gyü-lôh</rt><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>免掉</rb><rt>min-diao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>*gyiu*</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>*Ng*</rt><rb>免掉仔</rb><rt>*min-diao-ts*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>*cü-i*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>力道</rb><rt>lih-dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>难过</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>极头</rb><rt>gyih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>求勒</rb><rt>gyiu-leh</rt><rb>急</rb><rt>kyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>汗</rb><rt>ʽen</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>点打点</rb><rt>tin-tang-tin</rt><rb>凝拢了</rb><rt>nying-long-liao</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>渧落</rb><rt>ti-lôh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>求好了</rb><rt>Gyiu-hao-de</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>担</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>困熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>，<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>困熟东</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Ng ziah kʽeng, keh-go pe-ts teng Ngô do-ko. 或话，尔若肯，箇个杯子等我驮过。（原注）</sup>

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>*tao-de*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>领前</rb><rt>ling-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>嗅嘴</rb><rt>hyüong-cü</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做出来</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>动</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>Gyi-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>顺只</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽEo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>52</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇班</rb><rt>keh-pæn</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣殿里个</rb><rt>sing-din-li-go</rt><rb>侍卫</rb><rt>z-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>？<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇晌</rb><rt>keh-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt><rb>地方个</rb><rt>*di-fông-go*</rt><rb>势道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>带勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>衙门里</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>跟间</rb><rt>keng-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>明堂</rb><rt>Ming-dông</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>清盯</rb><rt>tsʽing-ting</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>57</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>58</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做队</rb><rt>tso-de</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt><rb>时辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt><rb>介相貌</rb><rt>ka-siang-mao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>着实</rb><rt>dziah-jih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>同队个</rb><rt>dong-de-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>60</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>懂</rb><rt>tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鸡</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>啼了</rb><rt>di-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>61</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>记得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>鸡</rb><rt>Kyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>啼个</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>三遭</rb><rt>sæn-tsao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>62</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>哭勒</rb><rt>kʽoh-leh</rt><rb>伤心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>63</sup> <ruby><rb>管守</rb><rt>Kwun-siu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>戏弄</rb><rt>hyi-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>64</sup> <ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>遮仔</rb><rt>tsô-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>巴掌</rb><rt>pô-công</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>讲讲</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>65</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>别样告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>66</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>TAO-LEH</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-liang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>*cong*</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>公会里</rb><rt>kong-we-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>*meng*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>67</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>68</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>69</sup> <ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>70</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>话着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>71</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>亲口</rb><rt>tsʽing-kʽeo</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>听闻了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Lk. XXIII.

<ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>靠实</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>摇惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禁住</rb><rt>kying-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>投</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>钱粮</rb><rt>zin-liang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>称</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>话着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>挑动</rb><rt>tʽiao-dong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咸到处</rb><rt>ʽen-tao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开台</rb><rt>kʽæ-dæ</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kyiao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>三个</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>该管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>长远了</rb><rt>dziang-yün-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>*Hyi-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拼命</rb><rt>pʽing-ming</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>嘲笑</rb><rt>tsao-siao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>戏弄</rb><rt>hyi-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一件</rb><rt>ih-gyin</rt><rb>雍雍</rb><rt>üong-üong</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>披勒</rb><rt>pʽi-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>怨气</rb><rt>ün-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>和拢了</rb><rt>ʽo-long-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>朝拢</rb><rt>dziao-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>摇惑</rb><rt>yiao-ʽôh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>当面</rb><rt>tông-min</rt><rb>审过了</rb><rt>sing-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>*dziu*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>*Hyi-lih*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>判断</rb><rt>*pʽun-tön*</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>*kʽen-læ*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>打过仔</rb><rt>tang-ko-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> （<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>节肯个</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>务必子</rb><rt>vu-pih-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。）<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>合口齐动</rb><rt>ʽeh-kʽeo-zi-dong</rt><rb>怀起来</rb><rt>wæ-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>Kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>*Pô-lah-pô*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>*teng*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一番</rb><rt>ih-fæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>怀起</rb><rt>wæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>！」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>恶</rb><rt>ôh</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看弗出</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>打过仔</rb><rt>tang-ko-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>逼</rb><rt>pih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>*Pe-lah-to*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>赢了</rb><rt>ying-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讨个</rb><rt>tʽao-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>断法</rb><rt>tön-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>讨个</rb><rt>tʽao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>造反</rb><rt>dzao-fæn</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>监里</rb><rt>kæn-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放掉</rb><rt>fông-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>*ping*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>*bæn*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>古利奈</rb><rt>Kwu-li-næ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>田畈里</rb><rt>din-pæn-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>抲勒</rb><rt>kʽô-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>还有星</rb><rt>wa-yiu-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>跟间</rb><rt>keng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>*hyüong-kwun-deo*</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭带转</rb><rt>nyin-tæn-cün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>耶路撒冷</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>Ve</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>生过</rb><rt>sang-ko</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奶</rb><rt>na</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>*na-hwun*</rt><rb>出过</rb><rt>cʽih-ko</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>！』<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>盖住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>！』<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>青</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>za</rt><rb>尚且</rb><rt>zông-tsʽia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>干</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>柴</rb><rt>*za*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>！」

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>拖勒去</rb><rt>tʽo-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>份齐</rb><rt>veng-zi</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>TSEO</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>骷髅</rb><rt>Kwʽu-leo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>饶赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>*gyi-zi*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Ziu-z* Kwʽu-lu-deo. 就是骷颅头。（原注）</sup>

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>看东</rb><rt>kʽen-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>Kwun-fu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>冷笑</rb><rt>lang-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>救过了</rb><rt>kyiu-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>拣着个</rb><rt>kæn-djôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>取笑</rb><rt>cʽü-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走拢去</rb><rt>tseo-long-kyʽi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>酸酒</rb><rt>sön-tsiu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>37</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一张</rb><rt>ih-tsiang</rt><rb>条子</rb><rt>diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希利尼</rb><rt>Hyi-li-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>罗马</rb><rt>Lo-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>希伯来</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>字眼</rb><rt>z-ngæn</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>KEH</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>NYING-GO</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>WÔNG-TI</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>挂间个</rb><rt>kwô-kæn-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>第二个</rb><rt>Di-nyi-go</rt><rb>应起来</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>定了</rb><rt>ding-leh</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>应该个</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>受个</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>Keh-we</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>做赚</rb><rt>tso-dzæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>执掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>国家个</rb><rt>koh-kô-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>记</rb><rt>kyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末仔</rb><rt>Kyih-mih-ts</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>福园</rb><rt>foh-yün</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>Jih-nyi</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>光景</rb><rt>kwông-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遍地</rb><rt>pin-di</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>*en*</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>变勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>圣殿里个</rb><rt>*sing*-din-li-go</rt><rb>幔帐</rb><rt>mun-tsiang</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>𠡒裂开</rb><rt>te-lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。』 」<ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>Kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>气</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>断了</rb><rt>dön-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-teh fæn, pin-tʽin-di-ʽô. Mt. Mk. yia z-ka. 有得翻，遍天地下。Mt. Mk. 也是介。（原注）</sup>

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>百总</rb><rt>Pah-tsong</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>Tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>敲敲</rb><rt>kʽao-kʽao</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>归去了</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相熟个</rb><rt>siang-joh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跟勒来个</rb><rt>keng-leh-læ-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>YIU</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>议事部里个</rb><rt>nyi-z-bu-li-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>商量</rb><rt>siang-liang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>徇情</rb><rt>shing-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚利马太</rb><rt>Üô-li-mô-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>神国</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>驮落来</rb><rt>do-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>细</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>裹好仔</rb><rt>ko-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>石岩</rb><rt>zah-ngæn</rt><rb>凿出来个</rb><rt>zôh-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>葬过</rb><rt>tsông-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>预备日</rb><rt>yü-be-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>葬法</rb><rt>tsông-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>归去</rb><rt>kyü-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>没药</rb><rt>meh-yiah</rt><rb>备好仔</rb><rt>be-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>庉过</rb><rt>deng-ko</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。

### Lk. XXIV.

<ruby><rb>七日个</rb><rt>Tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>头日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黑早</rb><rt>heh-tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>备好间个</rb><rt>be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>*meng*-kʽeo</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>翻过了</rb><rt>fæn-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>疑惑不决</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-peh-kyüih</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>雪刮亮</rb><rt>shih-kwah-liang</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>怕起来</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>伛倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>死人</rb><rt>Si-nying</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>活人</rb><rt>weh-nying</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 『<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>罪人个</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>钉煞</rb><rt>ting-sah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>*Nyü-nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>记得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>十一个</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约亚拿</rb><rt>Iah-üô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>*ah-nyiang*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>女人个</rb><rt>Nyü-nying-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt><rb>恶像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谣言</rb><rt>yiao-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立起身</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-sing</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坟墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>伛倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>安间</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>KEH</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>廿五</rb><rt>nyiæn-ng</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地名</rb><rt>di-ming</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以马忤</rb><rt>Yi-mô-ngwu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>依路</rb><rt>*i-lu*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲讲</rb><rt>kông-kông</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>近来</rb><rt>gying-læ</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>Tsing-hao</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>近拢去</rb><rt>gying-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>迷住了</rb><rt>mi-djü-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>忧闷相</rb><rt>iu-meng-siang</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>讲来讲去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革流巴</rb><rt>Keh-liu-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>耽搁间</rb><rt>tæn-kôh-kæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>*iao-bông*</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>城里个</rb><rt>zing-li-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>Kông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓个</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>总督</rb><rt>*tsong-toh*</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>想望</rb><rt>siang-vông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>kyih-mih</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup>
