<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>队伙</rb><rt>de-ho</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>果然</rb><rt>*ko-jün*</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>愚笨个</rb><rt>Nyü-beng-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>难得</rb><rt>næn-teh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>话过个</rb><rt>wô-ko-go</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>！<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>享</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>应该个</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>圣书里</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>详细</rb><rt>dziang-si</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>Kwʽa</rt><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>恶像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走过去</rb><rt>tseo-ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>煞扣</rb><rt>sah-kʽeo</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>*Yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>打笡</rb><rt>tang-tsʽia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>夜了</rb><rt>yia-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉东</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>坐席个</rb><rt>zo-zih-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福仔</rb><rt>coh-foh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>忽而然</rb><rt>hweh-r-jün</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗见</rb><rt>feh-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>Lu-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>热个</rb><rt>nyih-go</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>十一个</rb><rt>jih-ih-go</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>队伙</rb><rt>de-ho</rt><rb>聚拢间</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> 「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见过了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>路上个</rb><rt>lu-zông-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>话出来了</rb><rt>wô-cʽih-læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>正</rb><rt>Tsing</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>！」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃一惊</rb><rt>kyʽih-ih-kying</rt><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>定道</rb><rt>ding-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阴魂</rb><rt>ing-weng</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>懔懔动</rb><rt>ling-ling-dong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>疑惑个</rb><rt>nyi-ʽôh-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>？<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠实</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阴魂</rb><rt>ing-weng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>骨头</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮点</rb><rt>do-tin</rt><rb>炙熟了个</rb><rt>tsih-joh-liao-go</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>蜜蜂窠</rb><rt>mih-fong-kʽo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驮仔</rb><rt>do-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>吃了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-liao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先知人个</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>*shü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>圣诗</rb><rt>Sing-s</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>圣书个</rb><rt>Sing-shü-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>*kæ*</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt><rb>赦罪个</rb><rt>sô-ze-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>万国</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干个</rb><rt>z-ken-go</rt><rb>见证</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>应许个</rb><rt>ing-hyü-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大尼</rb><rt>Pah-da-nyi</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>提带起</rb><rt>di-tæn-kyʽi</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>正好</rb><rt>Tsing-hao</rt><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>赞美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>祝谢</rb><rt>coh-zia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
